Definitions: Mathlib.Order, part 3 of 4

399 definitions of 47 modules, Mathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice to Mathlib.Order.PartialSups. ‹ previous next ›

Show also:

Mathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice

BoundedLatticeHomstructureMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, type
Wording
bounded lattice homomorphism of x and x1 (from the name)
English
Let $x$ and $x_{1}$ be sets. Then the bounded lattice homomorphisms of $x$ and $x_{1}$ are a basic type.
Formula
$x , x_{1} \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soient $x$ et $x_{1}$ des ensembles. Alors les homomorphismes de treillis bornés de $x$ et $x_{1}$ sont un type de base.
BoundedLatticeHom.compdefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the composition of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$. Let $g$ be a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the composition of $f$ and $g$ is a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{2}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x_{1} , x_{2}), g \in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{comp} (f , g)\in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{2})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors la composition de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x$ et $x_{2}$.
BoundedLatticeHom.copydefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the copy of f and h (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Let $\mathrm{f'}$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $x_{1}$ such that $\mathrm{f'}$ is equal to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the copy of $f$ and $\mathrm{f'}$ is a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{1}), \mathrm{f'} \in x \rightarrow x_{1} , \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{f'}}{\operatorname{coe} (f)}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (f , \mathrm{f'} , h)\in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Soit $\mathrm{f'}$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des éléments de $x_{1}$ telle que $\mathrm{f'}$ soit égal à $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors le copy de $f$ et $\mathrm{f'}$ est un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x$ et $x_{1}$.
BoundedLatticeHom.dualdefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the dual (from the name)
English
The dual is an equivalence from the set of bounded lattice homomorphisms of $x$ and $x_{1}$ to the set of bounded lattice homomorphisms of the set of order duals of $x$ and the set of order duals of $x_{1}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{dual} \in \EquivMACRoAL {\mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})}{\mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (\mathrm{OrderDual} (x), \mathrm{OrderDual} (x_{1}))}$
French
Le dual est une équivalence de l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de treillis bornés de $x$ et $x_{1}$ à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de treillis bornés de l'ensemble de dual d'ordres de $x$ et l'ensemble de dual d'ordres de $x_{1}$.
BoundedLatticeHom.iddefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the identity of x (from the name)
English
Let $x$ be a set. Then the identity of $x$ is a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x$.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{id} (x)\in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x)$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Alors l'identité de $x$ est un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x$ et $x$.
BoundedLatticeHom.subtypeValdefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the subtype value of Psup and Pinf (from the name)
English
Assume that $P$ holds for the bottom. Assume that $P$ holds for the top. Assume that for all elements $x_{5}$ and $y_{1}$ of $x$, if $P$ holds for $x_{5}$, then if $P$ holds for $y_{1}$, then $P$ holds for $\MaxmaxMACRoAF {x_{5}}{y_{1}}$. Assume that for all elements $x_{4}$ and $y$ of $x$, if $P$ holds for $x_{4}$, then if $P$ holds for $y$, then $P$ holds for $\MinminMACRoAG {x_{4}}{y}$. Then the subtype value of $\mathrm{Psup}$ and $\mathrm{Pinf}$ is a bounded lattice homomorphism of $\SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{P (x_{3})}$ and $x$.
Formula
$P (\operatorname{bot}), P (\operatorname{top}), \forall x_{5} \in x . \forall y_{1} \in x . P (x_{5})\rightarrow P (y_{1})\rightarrow P (\MaxmaxMACRoAM {x_{5}}{y_{1}}), \forall x_{4} \in x . \forall y \in x . P (x_{4})\rightarrow P (y)\rightarrow P (\MinminMACRoAN {x_{4}}{y})\vdash \operatorname{subtypeVal} (\mathrm{Pbot} , \mathrm{Ptop} , \mathrm{Psup} , \mathrm{Pinf})\in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (\SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{P (x_{3})}, x)$
French
Supposons que $P$ tient pour le bottom. Supposons que $P$ tient pour le top. Supposons que pour tous les éléments $x_{5}$ et $y_{1}$ de $x$, si $P$ tient pour $x_{5}$, alors si $P$ tient pour $y_{1}$, alors $P$ tient pour $\MaxmaxMACRoAF {x_{5}}{y_{1}}$. Supposons que pour tous les éléments $x_{4}$ et $y$ de $x$, si $P$ tient pour $x_{4}$, alors si $P$ tient pour $y$, alors $P$ tient pour $\MinminMACRoAG {x_{4}}{y}$. Alors la valeur de subtype de $\mathrm{Psup}$ et $\mathrm{Pinf}$ est un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $\SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{P (x_{3})}$ et $x$.
BoundedLatticeHom.toBoundedOrderHomdefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the bounded order homomorphism of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the bounded order homomorphism of $f$ is a bounded order homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{toBoundedOrderHom} (f)\in \mathrm{BoundedOrderHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors l'homomorphisme d'ordres borné de $f$ est un homomorphisme d'ordres borné de $x$ et $x_{1}$.
BoundedLatticeHom.toInfTopHomdefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the infimum top homomorphism of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the infimum top homomorphism of $\mathrm{self}$ is an infimum top homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $\operatorname{toSupHom}$ applied to $\operatorname{toLatticeHom}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $\mathrm{LatticeHom\_\_map\_inf'}$ applied to $\operatorname{toLatticeHom}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $\mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom\_\_map\_top'}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{toInfTopHom} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{toFun} (\operatorname{toSupHom} (\operatorname{toLatticeHom} (\mathrm{self}))), \mathrm{LatticeHom\_\_map\_inf'} (\operatorname{toLatticeHom} (\mathrm{self}))), \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom\_\_map\_top'} (\mathrm{self}))\in \mathrm{InfTopHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors l'homomorphisme supérieur d'infimums de $\mathrm{self}$ est un homomorphisme supérieur d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toSupHom}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toLatticeHom}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $\mathrm{LatticeHom\_\_map\_inf'}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toLatticeHom}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $\mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom\_\_map\_top'}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.
BoundedLatticeHom.toSupBotHomdefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the supremum bottom homomorphism of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a bounded lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the supremum bottom homomorphism of $\mathrm{self}$ is a supremum bottom homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{toSupHom}$ applied to $\operatorname{toLatticeHom}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $\mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom\_\_map\_bot'}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{toSupBotHom} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{toSupHom} (\operatorname{toLatticeHom} (\mathrm{self})), \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom\_\_map\_bot'} (\mathrm{self}))\in \mathrm{SupBotHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ un homomorphisme de treillis borné de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors l'homomorphisme inférieur de supremums de $\mathrm{self}$ est un homomorphisme inférieur de supremums de $x$ et $x_{1}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toSupHom}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toLatticeHom}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $\mathrm{BoundedLatticeHom\_\_map\_bot'}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.
BoundedLatticeHomClassclassMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $F$, $x$ and $x_{1}$ be sets. Then we can say that $\mathrm{BoundedLatticeHomClass}$ holds for $F$, $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$F , x , x_{1} \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{BoundedLatticeHomClass} (F , x , x_{1})\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soient $F$, $x$ et $x_{1}$ des ensembles. Alors nous pouvons dire que $\mathrm{BoundedLatticeHomClass}$ tient pour $F$, $x$ et $x_{1}$.

InfTopHom.compdefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the composition of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be an infimum top homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$. Let $g$ be an infimum top homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the composition of $f$ and $g$ is an infimum top homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{2}$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the composition of $\operatorname{toInfHom}$ applied to $f$ and $\operatorname{toInfHom}$ applied to $g$ and $\mathrm{TopHom\_\_map\_top'}$ applied to the composition of the top homomorphism of $f$ and the top homomorphism of $g$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{InfTopHom} (x_{1} , x_{2}), g \in \mathrm{InfTopHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{comp} (f , g):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{toInfHom} (f), \operatorname{toInfHom} (g)), \mathrm{TopHom\_\_map\_top'} (\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{toTopHom} (f), \operatorname{toTopHom} (g))))\in \mathrm{InfTopHom} (x , x_{2})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme supérieur d'infimums de $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme supérieur d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors la composition de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme supérieur d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{2}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à la composition de $\operatorname{toInfHom}$ appliqué à $f$ et $\operatorname{toInfHom}$ appliqué à $g$ et $\mathrm{TopHom\_\_map\_top'}$ appliqué à la composition de l'homomorphisme supérieur de $f$ et l'homomorphisme supérieur de $g$.

Wording
the subtype value of Ptop and Pinf (from the name)

InfTopHom.toTopHomdefMathlib.Order.Hom.BoundedLattice, function or constant
Wording
the top homomorphism of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be an infimum top homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the top homomorphism of $\mathrm{self}$ is a top homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $\operatorname{toInfHom}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $\mathrm{InfTopHom\_\_map\_top'}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{InfTopHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{toTopHom} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{toFun} (\operatorname{toInfHom} (\mathrm{self})), \mathrm{InfTopHom\_\_map\_top'} (\mathrm{self}))\in \mathrm{TopHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ un homomorphisme supérieur d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors l'homomorphisme supérieur de $\mathrm{self}$ est un homomorphisme supérieur de $x$ et $x_{1}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toInfHom}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $\mathrm{InfTopHom\_\_map\_top'}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.

$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{SupBotHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{toBotHom} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{toFun} (\operatorname{toSupHom} (\mathrm{self})), \mathrm{SupBotHom\_\_map\_bot'} (\mathrm{self}))\in \mathrm{BotHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ un homomorphisme inférieur de supremums de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors l'homomorphisme inférieur de $\mathrm{self}$ est un homomorphisme inférieur de $x$ et $x_{1}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toSupHom}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $\mathrm{SupBotHom\_\_map\_bot'}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.

CompleteLatticeHom.OrderIso.toCompleteLatticeHomdefMathlib.Order.Hom.CompleteLattice, function or constant
Wording
the complete lattice homomorphism of f (from the name)
English

Formula

Wording
the composition of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a complete lattice homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$. Let $g$ be a complete lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the composition of $f$ and $g$ is a complete lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{2}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{CompleteLatticeHom} (x_{1} , x_{2}), g \in \mathrm{CompleteLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{comp} (f , g)\in \mathrm{CompleteLatticeHom} (x , x_{2})$
French

CompleteLatticeHom.copydefMathlib.Order.Hom.CompleteLattice, function or constant
Wording
the copy of f and h (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a complete lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Let $\mathrm{f'}$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $x_{1}$ such that $\mathrm{f'}$ is equal to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the copy of $f$ and $\mathrm{f'}$ is a complete lattice homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{CompleteLatticeHom} (x , x_{1}), \mathrm{f'} \in x \rightarrow x_{1} , \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{f'}}{\operatorname{coe} (f)}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (f , \mathrm{f'} , h)\in \mathrm{CompleteLatticeHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de treillis complet de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Soit $\mathrm{f'}$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des éléments de $x_{1}$ telle que $\mathrm{f'}$ soit égal à $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors le copy de $f$ et $\mathrm{f'}$ est un homomorphisme de treillis complet de $x$ et $x_{1}$.

Mathlib.Order.Hom.Lattice

InfHomstructureMathlib.Order.Hom.Lattice, type
Wording
infimum homomorphism of x and x1 (from the name)
English
Let $x$ and $x_{1}$ be sets. Then the infimum homomorphisms of $x$ and $x_{1}$ are a basic type.
Formula
$x , x_{1} \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{InfHom} (x , x_{1})\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soient $x$ et $x_{1}$ des ensembles. Alors les homomorphismes d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$ sont un type de base.
InfHom.compdefMathlib.Order.Hom.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
the composition of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be an infimum homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$. Let $g$ be an infimum homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the composition of $f$ and $g$ is an infimum homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{2}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{InfHom} (x_{1} , x_{2}), g \in \mathrm{InfHom} (x , x_{1})\vdash \operatorname{comp} (f , g)\in \mathrm{InfHom} (x , x_{2})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'infimums de $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors la composition de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{2}$.
InfHom.constdefMathlib.Order.Hom.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
the constant function of x and b (from the docstring)
English
Let $x$ be a set. Let $b$ be an element of $x_{1}$. Then the constant function of $x$ and $b$ is an infimum homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} , b \in x_{1} \vdash \operatorname{const} (x , b)\in \mathrm{InfHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Soit $b$ un élément de $x_{1}$. Alors la fonction constante de $x$ et $b$ est un homomorphisme d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$.
InfHom.copydefMathlib.Order.Hom.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
the copy of f and h (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be an infimum homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Let $\mathrm{f'}$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $x_{1}$ such that $\mathrm{f'}$ is equal to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the copy of $f$ and $\mathrm{f'}$ is an infimum homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{InfHom} (x , x_{1}), \mathrm{f'} \in x \rightarrow x_{1} , \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{f'}}{\operatorname{coe} (f)}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (f , \mathrm{f'} , h)\in \mathrm{InfHom} (x , x_{1})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$. Soit $\mathrm{f'}$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des éléments de $x_{1}$ telle que $\mathrm{f'}$ soit égal à $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors le copy de $f$ et $\mathrm{f'}$ est un homomorphisme d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$.
InfHom.dualdefMathlib.Order.Hom.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
the dual (from the name)
English
The dual is an equivalence from the set of infimum homomorphisms of $x$ and $x_{1}$ to the set of supremum homomorphisms of the set of order duals of $x$ and the set of order duals of $x_{1}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{dual} \in \EquivMACRoAL {\mathrm{InfHom} (x , x_{1})}{\mathrm{SupHom} (\mathrm{OrderDual} (x), \mathrm{OrderDual} (x_{1}))}$
French
Le dual est une équivalence de l'ensemble d'homomorphismes d'infimums de $x$ et $x_{1}$ à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de supremums de l'ensemble de dual d'ordres de $x$ et l'ensemble de dual d'ordres de $x_{1}$.

InfHom.subtypeValdefMathlib.Order.Hom.Lattice, function or constant
Wording
the subtype value of Pinf (from the name)
English
Assume that for all elements $x_{3}$ and $y$ of $x$, if $P$ holds for $x_{3}$, then if $P$ holds for $y$, then $P$ holds for $\MinminMACRoAG {x_{3}}{y}$. Then the subtype value of $\mathrm{Pinf}$ is an infimum homomorphism of $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{P (x_{2})}$ and $x$.
Formula
$\forall x_{3} \in x . \forall y \in x . P (x_{3})\rightarrow P (y)\rightarrow P (\MinminMACRoAN {x_{3}}{y})\vdash \operatorname{subtypeVal} (\mathrm{Pinf})\in \mathrm{InfHom} (\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{P (x_{2})}, x)$
French

Order.CofinalstructureMathlib.Order.Ideal, type
Wording
no wording
English
Let $P$ be a set. Then the elements of $\operatorname{Cofinal}$ applied to $P$ are a basic type.
Formula
$P \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{Cofinal} (P)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $P$ un ensemble. Alors les éléments de $\operatorname{Cofinal}$ appliqué à $P$ sont un type de base.
Order.Cofinal.abovedefMathlib.Order.Ideal, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $D$ be an element of $\operatorname{Cofinal}$ applied to $P$. Let $x_{1}$ be an element of $P$. Then $\operatorname{above}$ applied to $D$ and $x_{1}$ is an element of $P$ defined as $\operatorname{choose}$ applied to $\operatorname{isCofinal}$ applied to $D$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$D \in \operatorname{Cofinal} (P), x_{1} \in P \vdash \operatorname{above} (D , x_{1}):= \operatorname{choose} (\operatorname{isCofinal} (D , x_{1}))\in P$
French
Soit $D$ un élément de $\operatorname{Cofinal}$ appliqué à $P$. Soit $x_{1}$ un élément de $P$. Alors $\operatorname{above}$ appliqué à $D$ et $x_{1}$ est un élément de $P$ défini comme $\operatorname{choose}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{isCofinal}$ appliqué à $D$ et $x_{1}$.
Order.Cofinal.carrierdefMathlib.Order.Ideal, function or constant
Wording
the carrier of self (from the docstring)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be an element of $\operatorname{Cofinal}$ applied to $P$. Then the carrier of $\mathrm{self}$ is an element of $\wp P$ defined as $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{Cofinal} (P)\vdash \operatorname{carrier} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{proj0} (\mathrm{self})\in \wp P$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ un élément de $\operatorname{Cofinal}$ appliqué à $P$. Alors le carrier de $\mathrm{self}$ est un élément de $\wp P$ défini comme $\operatorname{proj0}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.

Mathlib.Order.Monotone.Defs

AntitonedefMathlib.Order.Monotone.Defs, predicate
Wording
f is a antitone (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $x_{1}$. Then $f$ is an antitone, if for all elements $a$ and $b$ of $x$, if $a \leq b$, then $f (b)\leq f (a)$.
Formula
$f \in x \rightarrow x_{1} \vdash \mathrm{Antitone} (f):\Leftrightarrow \forall a \in x . \forall b \in x . a \leq b \rightarrow f (b)\leq f (a)$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des éléments de $x_{1}$. Alors $f$ est un antitone si pour tous les éléments $a$ et $b$ de $x$, si $a \leq b$, alors $f (b)\leq f (a)$.

Wording

English

$\operatorname{infGi}$ is a Galois coinsertion of the function that maps $x_{1}$ to $\operatorname{toDual}$ applied to $\operatorname{principal}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and the function that maps $F$ to the infimum of $\operatorname{ofDual}$ applied to $F$.
Formula
$\operatorname{infGi} \in \mathrm{GaloisCoinsertion} (\lambda x_{1} . \operatorname{toDual} (\operatorname{principal} (x_{1})), \lambda F . \operatorname{sInf} (\operatorname{ofDual} (F)))$
French
$\operatorname{infGi}$ est une coinsertion de Galois de la fonction qui envoie $x_{1}$ à $\operatorname{toDual}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{principal}$ appliqué à $x_{1}$ et la fonction qui envoie $F$ à l'infimum de $\operatorname{ofDual}$ appliqué à $F$.
Order.PFilter.principaldefMathlib.Order.PFilter, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $p$ be an element of $P$. Then $\operatorname{principal}$ applied to $p$ is a filter of $P$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{principal}$ applied to $\operatorname{toDual}$ applied to $p$.
Formula
$p \in P \vdash \operatorname{principal} (p):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{principal} (\operatorname{toDual} (p)))\in \operatorname{PFilter} (P)$
French
Soit $p$ un élément de $P$. Alors $\operatorname{principal}$ appliqué à $p$ est un filtre de $P$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{principal}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toDual}$ appliqué à $p$.

Mathlib.Order.Part

OrderHom.partBinddefMathlib.Order.Part, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be an order homomorphism of $x$ and the set of parts of $x_{1}$. Let $g$ be an order homomorphism of $x$ and the set of functions from elements of $x_{1}$ to parts of $x_{2}$. Then $\operatorname{partBind}$ applied to $f$ and $g$ is an order homomorphism of $x$ and the set of parts of $x_{2}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{OrderHom} (x , \mathrm{Part} (x_{1})), g \in \mathrm{OrderHom} (x , x_{1} \rightarrow \mathrm{Part} (x_{2}))\vdash \operatorname{partBind} (f , g)\in \mathrm{OrderHom} (x , \mathrm{Part} (x_{2}))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'ordres de $x$ et l'ensemble de parties de $x_{1}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme d'ordres de $x$ et l'ensemble de fonctions des éléments de $x_{1}$ à des parties de $x_{2}$. Alors $\operatorname{partBind}$ appliqué à $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme d'ordres de $x$ et l'ensemble de parties de $x_{2}$.

Mathlib.Order.PartialSups

partialSupsdefMathlib.Order.PartialSups, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $x_{1}$ to elements of $x$. Then $\mathrm{partialSups}$ applied to $f$ is an order homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $x$.
Formula
$f \in x_{1} \rightarrow x \vdash \mathrm{partialSups} (f)\in \mathrm{OrderHom} (x_{1} , x)$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $x_{1}$ à des éléments de $x$. Alors $\mathrm{partialSups}$ appliqué à $f$ est un homomorphisme d'ordres de $x_{1}$ et $x$.
partialSups.gidefMathlib.Order.PartialSups, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
$\operatorname{gi}$ is a Galois insertion of $\mathrm{partialSups}$ and $\operatorname{coe}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{gi} \in \mathrm{GaloisInsertion} (\mathrm{partialSups} , \operatorname{coe})$
French
$\operatorname{gi}$ est une insertion de Galois de $\mathrm{partialSups}$ et $\operatorname{coe}$.
